(1.17:1)\", desc: 'Ultra-compact smart-beeper format' },\n      \"154\": { name: '1.54\" Square Matrix',   w: 240, h: 240, scale: 2.2, ratio: \"1:1\", desc: 'Perfect square talisman display' },\n      \"096\": { name: '0.96\" Micro OLED',      w: 128, h: 64,  scale: 4.0, ratio: \"2:1\", desc: 'SSD1306 hacker terminal display' }\n    };\n\n    // Shared Accent Pool for Quote Marks & Container\n    const ACCENT_POOL = [\n      { name: \"Sapphire Blue\", hex: \"#38bdf8\" },\n      { name: \"Imperial Gold\", hex: \"#f59e0b\" },\n      { name: \"Emerald Mint\",  hex: \"#10b981\" },\n      { name: \"Crimson Rose\",  hex: \"#f43f5e\" }\n    ];\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 2. STATE REPOSITORY\n    // ==============================================================================\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n\n    let currentPresetKey = '19';\n    let currentTheme = 'obsidian';\n    let showContext = true;\n    let isNative1x = false;\n    let reRollSeed = 0;\n    let autoScrollInterval = null;\n    let soundEnabled = true;\n\n    // Web Audio Synthesizer (Zero-Asset 8-bit Pager Chirp)\n    let audioCtx = null;\n    function playBeeperChirp(type = 'chirp') {\n      if (!soundEnabled) return;\n      try {\n        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();\n        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();\n\n        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();\n        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();\n        osc.connect(gain);\n        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);\n\n        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;\n        if (type === 'chirp') {\n          osc.type = 'square';\n          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1760, now); // A6\n          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(2637, now + 0.04); // E7\n          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, now);\n          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.1);\n          osc.start(now);\n          osc.stop(now + 0.1);\n        } else if (type === 'click') {\n          osc.type = 'triangle';\n          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(987, now);\n          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.03, now);\n          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.03);\n          osc.start(now);\n          osc.stop(now + 0.03);\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        // AudioContext silent fallback\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleSound() {\n      soundEnabled = !soundEnabled;\n      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-sound');\n      btn.textContent = soundEnabled ? '🔔 Sound: ON' : '🔕 Sound: OFF';\n      btn.classList.toggle('active', soundEnabled);\n      if (soundEnabled) playBeeperChirp('chirp');\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 3. INITIALIZATION & DATA FETCHING\n    // ==============================================================================\n    async function init() {\n      applyPreset('19');\n\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => null),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => null)\n        ]);\n\n        if (taxRes && qRes && qRes.length > 0) {\n          availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n          availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n          quotesList = qRes;\n          document.getElementById('connection-status').innerHTML = '● LIVE API SYNCED';\n          document.getElementById('connection-status').style.color = '#38bdf8';\n        } else {\n          useFallbackData();\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.warn(\"Using offline fallback data:\", err);\n        useFallbackData();\n      }\n\n      populateSelect();\n\n      // Default to Quote #6 (or first)\n      const target = quotesList.find(q => (q.idx === 6 || q.index === 6)) || quotesList[0];\n      if (target) {\n        loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function useFallbackData() {\n      quotesList = FALLBACK_QUOTES;\n      document.getElementById('connection-status').innerHTML = '● STANDALONE CACHE';\n      document.getElementById('connection-status').style.color = '#f59e0b';\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = (q.main_content || \"\").split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 32);\n        const idx = q.idx ?? q.index ?? '?';\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${idx} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      playBeeperChirp('chirp');\n      renderActivePrescript();\n    }\n\n    function navQuote(step) {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const curIdx = quotesList.findIndex(q => q.id === (activeQuote ? activeQuote.id : ''));\n      let nextIdx = (curIdx + step + quotesList.length) % quotesList.length;\n      loadSelectedQuote(quotesList[nextIdx].id);\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    function reRollColors() {\n      reRollSeed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);\n      playBeeperChirp('click');\n      renderActivePrescript();\n    }\n\n    function toggleContext() {\n      showContext = !showContext;\n      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-context');\n      btn.textContent = `Context: ${showContext ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;\n      btn.classList.toggle('active', showContext);\n      playBeeperChirp('click');\n      renderActivePrescript();\n    }\n\n    function setTheme(tName) {\n      currentTheme = tName;\n      ['obsidian', 'eink', 'amber', 'matrix'].forEach(t => {\n        const b = document.getElementById(`btn-theme-` + t);\n        if (b) b.classList.toggle('active', t === tName);\n      });\n      const sc = document.getElementById('epaper-screen');\n      if (sc) sc.className = `epaper-screen theme-${tName}`;\n      playBeeperChirp('click');\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 4. SCREEN PRESET & RATIO APPLICATION\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function applyPreset(key) {\n      currentPresetKey = key;\n      const p = HARDWARE_PRESETS[key] || HARDWARE_PRESETS['19'];\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.ratio-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`rb-${key}`);\n      if (activeBtn) activeBtn.classList.add('active');\n\n      document.getElementById('active-screen-spec').textContent = `${p.name} · ${p.w}×${p.h} px · Ratio ${p.ratio}`;\n      document.getElementById('bezel-dim-label').textContent = `${p.w} × ${p.h} NATIVE`;\n\n      const screen = document.getElementById('epaper-screen');\n      const scaleMultiplier = isNative1x ? 1.0 : p.scale;\n\n      const targetW = Math.round(p.w * scaleMultiplier);\n      const targetH = Math.round(p.h * scaleMultiplier);\n\n      screen.style.width = `${targetW}px`;\n      screen.style.height = `${targetH}px`;\n      screen.style.aspectRatio = `${p.w} / ${p.h}`;\n\n      playBeeperChirp('click');\n    }\n\n    function toggleNativeScale() {\n      isNative1x = !isNative1x;\n      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-scale');\n      btn.textContent = isNative1x ? 'Scale: 1:1 Native' : 'Scale: Studio 2x';\n      btn.classList.toggle('active', isNative1x);\n      applyPreset(currentPresetKey);\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 5. VIEWPORT SCROLL ENGINE (ZERO-CRAM FLOW)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function scrollViewport(offset) {\n      const vp = document.getElementById('ep-viewport');\n      if (vp) {\n        vp.scrollBy({ top: offset, behavior: 'smooth' });\n        playBeeperChirp('click');\n      }\n    }\n\n    function scrollViewportTo(pos) {\n      const vp = document.getElementById('ep-viewport');\n      if (vp) {\n        if (pos === 'top') vp.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });\n        if (pos === 'bottom') vp.scrollTo({ top: vp.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth' });\n        playBeeperChirp('click');\n      }\n    }\n\n    function updateScrollTelemetry() {\n      const vp = document.getElementById('ep-viewport');\n      const label = document.getElementById('scroll-pos-label');\n      if (!vp || !label) return;\n\n      const maxScroll = vp.scrollHeight - vp.clientHeight;\n      if (maxScroll <= 0) {\n        label.textContent = \"Scroll: Fitted 100%\";\n      } else {\n        const pct = Math.round((vp.scrollTop / maxScroll) * 100);\n        label.textContent = `Scroll: ${pct}%`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleAutoScroll() {\n      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-autoscroll');\n      if (autoScrollInterval) {\n        clearInterval(autoScrollInterval);\n        autoScrollInterval = null;\n        btn.textContent = 'Auto-Scroll: OFF';\n        btn.classList.remove('active');\n      } else {\n        btn.textContent = 'Auto-Scroll: ON';\n        btn.classList.add('active');\n        autoScrollInterval = setInterval(() => {\n          const vp = document.getElementById('ep-viewport');\n          if (vp) {\n            if (vp.scrollTop + vp.clientHeight >= vp.scrollHeight - 1) {\n              vp.scrollTop = 0;\n            } else {\n              vp.scrollTop += 1;\n            }\n          }\n        }, 40);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 6. METADATA NORMALIZATION & COLOR GENERATOR\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function generateTagColorStyle(tagName, tagIndex) {\n      let hash = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < tagName.length; i++) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + tagName.charCodeAt(i);\n        hash |= 0;\n      }\n      const goldenAngle = 137.508;\n      const hue = Math.abs(Math.floor((hash * goldenAngle + tagIndex * 89 + reRollSeed) % 360));\n\n      const textColor   = `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 72%)`;\n      const borderColor = `hsla(${hue}, 70%, 40%, 0.45)`;\n      const bgColor     = `hsla(${hue}, 65%, 8%, 0.9)`;\n      return `background: ${bgColor} !important; border-color: ${borderColor} !important; color: ${textColor} !important;`;\n    }\n\n    function resolveBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = q.specificName || (specific ? specific.name : \"—\");\n      const containerName = q.containerName || (specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\"));\n\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || q.effectiveDate || \"\").trim();\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n      }\n\n      let hashtagThemes = q.hashtagThemes || [];\n      if (hashtagThemes.length === 0 && q.theme_ids) {\n        hashtagThemes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n          const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n          return th ? `#${th.name}` : `#${tid}`;\n        });\n      }\n      if (hashtagThemes.length === 0) hashtagThemes = [\"#Wisdom\"];\n\n      const rawCategory = q.category || (specific ? specific.category : \"\") || \"\";\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index ?? 1,\n        content,\n        context,\n        category: rawCategory,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        hashtagThemes\n      };\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 7. RENDER COMPONENT\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderActivePrescript() {\n      const b = resolveBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n      const screen = document.getElementById('epaper-screen');\n\n      // Alignment: writing => justified; music/poem => left\n      const catLower = (b.category || \"\").toLowerCase().trim();\n      const isWriting = catLower === \"writing\" || catLower === \"bài viết\" || catLower === \"baiviet\";\n      const alignClass = isWriting ? \"content-justify\" : \"content-left\";\n\n      // Quotation Mark & Container Accent (Synchronized)\n      const quoteSeed = (typeof b.idx === 'number' ? b.idx : (parseInt(String(b.idx).replace(/\\D/g, ''), 10) || 1)) + reRollSeed;\n      const accentIndex = Math.abs(quoteSeed % ACCENT_POOL.length);\n      const activeAccent = ACCENT_POOL[accentIndex];\n      const sharedAccentColor = activeAccent.hex;\n\n      // Format Content with quotes\n      const formattedContent = `<span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-open\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">“</span><em>${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}</em><span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-close\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">”</span>`;\n\n      // Header Components\n      const headerTitleHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-left\"><strong class=\"ep-idx-num\">#${b.idx}</strong><span class=\"ep-sep\">~</span><span class=\"ep-specific-title\">${b.specificName}</span></span>`;\n      const headerDateHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-right\"><span style=\"color:#555;\">Date:</span><span class=\"ep-date-val\">${b.effectiveDate}</span></span>`;\n\n      // Tags Blocks\n      const tagsBlocks = b.hashtagThemes.map((t, idx) => {\n        const tagStyle = generateTagColorStyle(t, idx);\n        return `<span class=\"ep-tag-block\" style=\"${tagStyle}\">${t}</span>`;\n      }).join('');\n\n      // Container Name\n      const containerHTML = `<span class=\"ep-container-name\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">${b.containerName}</span>`;\n\n      // Render Screen with FIXED HEADER + SCROLLABLE VIEWPORT + FIXED FOOTER\n      screen.innerHTML = `\n        <!-- ZONE 1: FIXED HARDWARE HEADER -->\n        <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n          <div>${headerTitleHTML}</div>\n          <div>${headerDateHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- ZONE 2 & 3: SCROLLABLE VIEWPORT (ZERO-CRAM CORE) -->\n        <div class=\"ep-viewport\" id=\"ep-viewport\" onscroll=\"updateScrollTelemetry()\">\n          <div class=\"ep-content ${alignClass}\">\n            ${formattedContent}\n          </div>\n\n          ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n            <div class=\"ep-context\">\n              ${b.context}\n            </div>\n          ` : ''}\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- ZONE 4: FIXED HARDWARE FOOTER -->\n        <div class=\"ep-footer\">\n          <div class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsBlocks}</div>\n          <div class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n      `;\n\n      // Reset scroll position and update telemetry\n      const vp = document.getElementById('ep-viewport');\n      if (vp) {\n        vp.scrollTop = 0;\n        setTimeout(updateScrollTelemetry, 50);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Keyboard Shortcuts (Arrow keys & Space for tactile scrolling)\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n      if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'SELECT') return;\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { scrollViewport(-40); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { scrollViewport(40); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.key === 'PageUp') { scrollViewport(-140); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.key === 'PageDown' || e.key === ' ') { scrollViewport(140); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { navQuote(-1); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { navQuote(1); e.preventDefault(); }\n    });\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}

Chunk quote lines into 4-line pages\n      const rawQuotePages = [];\n      for (let i = 0; i < rawQuoteLines.length; i += MAX_LINES) {\n        rawQuotePages.push(rawQuoteLines.slice(i, i + MAX_LINES));\n      }\n      if (rawQuotePages.length === 0) rawQuotePages.push(['']);\n\n      const totalQuotePages = rawQuotePages.length;\n\n      rawQuotePages.forEach((lines, pIdx) => {\n        const isFirst = (pIdx === 0);\n        const isLastQuotePage = (pIdx === totalQuotePages - 1);\n        const decoratedLines = lines.map(l => l);\n\n        // Decorate opening quote on page 1\n        if (isFirst && decoratedLines.length > 0) {\n          decoratedLines[0] = `<span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-open\" style=\"color:${accentColor};\">“</span>${decoratedLines[0]}`;\n        } else if (!isFirst && decoratedLines.length > 0) {\n          decoratedLines[0] = `<span class=\"ep-ellipsis-cue\" style=\"color:${accentColor};\">... </span>${decoratedLines[0]}`;\n        }\n\n        // Decorate line 4 (or end of page)\n        const lastLineIdx = decoratedLines.length - 1;\n        if (!isLastQuotePage) {\n          // Quote continues onto next page: indicate with \"...\"\n          decoratedLines[lastLineIdx] = `${decoratedLines[lastLineIdx]}<span class=\"ep-ellipsis-cue\" style=\"color:${accentColor};\">...</span>`;\n        } else {\n          // Quote finishes on this page: append closing quote\n          decoratedLines[lastLineIdx] = `${decoratedLines[lastLineIdx]}<span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-close\" style=\"color:${accentColor};\">”</span>`;\n        }\n\n        pages.push({\n          type: 'quote',\n          lines: decoratedLines,\n          hasNext: !isLastQuotePage\n        });\n      });\n\n      // 2. {"Edit":{"file_path":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\.claude\\worktrees\\rien-prescript-beeper-fixes\\Entropy\\Rose\\esp32_19_display_mock.html","adds":"    // Helper to justify a list of words across targetWidth in monospace text\n    function justifyWordsToWidth(words, targetWidth) {\n      if (!words || words.length === 0) return ' '.repeat(targetWidth);\n      if (words.length === 1) return words[0].padEnd(targetWidth, ' ');\n      const totalLetters = words.reduce((acc, w) => acc + w.length, 0);\n      const gaps = words.length - 1;\n      const totalSpaces = targetWidth - totalLetters;\n      if (totalSpaces < gaps) return words.join(' ').padEnd(targetWidth, ' ');\n      const spacesPerGap = Math.floor(totalSpaces / gaps);\n      const extraSpaces = totalSpaces % gaps;\n      let res = '';\n      for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {\n        res += words[i];\n        if (i < gaps) {\n          const spCount = spacesPerGap + (i < extraSpaces ? 1 : 0);\n          res += ' '.repeat(spCount);\n        }\n      }\n      return res;\n    }\n\n    // Splits quote & context into discrete pages of AT MOST 4 LINES each with strict character budgeting\n    function buildPrescriptPages(bundle, maxChars, accentColor) {\n      const content = (bundle.content || \"\").trim();\n      const pages = [];\n      const MAX_LINES = 4;\n\n      if (!content) return [{ type: 'quote', lines: [''], hasNext: false }];\n\n      // Tokenize by paragraphs first to honor explicit line breaks (\\n)\n      const paragraphs = content.split('\\n');\n      const tokens = [];\n      for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {\n        const pText = paragraphs[i].trim();\n        if (pText) {\n          const words = pText.split(/\\s+/);\n          for (const w of words) tokens.push(w);\n        }\n        if (i < paragraphs.length - 1) {\n          tokens.push('\\n');\n        }\n      }\n\n      let tokenIdx = 0;\n      let pageNum = 0;\n\n      while (tokenIdx < tokens.length) {\n        pageNum++;\n        const isFirstPage = (pageNum === 1);\n        const pageLineWordGroups = [];\n\n        for (let lineNum = 1; lineNum <= MAX_LINES && tokenIdx < tokens.length; lineNum++) {\n          const isLine4 = (lineNum === MAX_LINES);\n\n          // Budget characters for this line:\n          // Line 1 reserves 1 char for “\n          // Line 4 reserves 3 chars for ... when text continues\n          let lineBudget = maxChars;\n          if (isFirstPage && lineNum === 1) lineBudget -= 1;\n          if (isLine4) lineBudget -= 3;\n\n          const lineWords = [];\n          let curLen = 0;\n\n          while (tokenIdx < tokens.length) {\n            const nextTok = tokens[tokenIdx];\n            if (nextTok === '\\n') {\n              tokenIdx++;\n              break;\n            }\n            const testLen = curLen === 0 ? nextTok.length : curLen + 1 + nextTok.length;\n            if (testLen <= lineBudget) {\n              lineWords.push(nextTok);\n              curLen = testLen;\n              tokenIdx++;\n            } else {\n              break;\n            }\n          }\n\n          pageLineWordGroups.push(lineWords);\n        }\n\n        // Lookahead to check if real words remain\n        let lookAheadIdx = tokenIdx;\n        while (lookAheadIdx < tokens.length && tokens[lookAheadIdx] === '\\n') {\n          lookAheadIdx++;\n        }\n        const hasMoreQuote = (lookAheadIdx < tokens.length);\n\n        // Decorate opening quote on line 1 and closing quote / ellipsis on last line\n        if (isFirstPage && pageLineWordGroups.length > 0 && pageLineWordGroups[0].length > 0) {\n          pageLineWordGroups[0][0] = `“${pageLineWordGroups[0][0]}`;\n        }\n        const lastGrpIdx = pageLineWordGroups.length - 1;\n        if (lastGrpIdx >= 0 && pageLineWordGroups[lastGrpIdx].length > 0) {\n          const lastWordIdx = pageLineWordGroups[lastGrpIdx].length - 1;\n          if (hasMoreQuote) {\n            pageLineWordGroups[lastGrpIdx][lastWordIdx] += '...';\n          } else {\n            pageLineWordGroups[lastGrpIdx][lastWordIdx] += '”';\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Format line strings with category-driven alignment (writing => justified, music/poem => left)\n        const catNorm = (bundle.category || '').toLowerCase().trim();\n        const isWriting = catNorm === 'writing' || catNorm === 'bài viết' || catNorm === 'baiviet';\n\n        const lineObjs = pageLineWordGroups.map((words, lIdx) => {\n          const isLastLineOfQuote = (!hasMoreQuote && lIdx === pageLineWordGroups.length - 1);\n          let rowStr = '';\n          if (isWriting && !isLastLineOfQuote && words.length > 1) {\n            rowStr = justifyWordsToWidth(words, maxChars);\n          } else {\n            rowStr = words.join(' ').padEnd(maxChars, ' ');\n          }\n          return {\n            text: rowStr,\n            align: isWriting && !isLastLineOfQuote ?
